#### T08-flow-1 · A focus preview covers a neighbouring control and takes its first click

- **State**: code-verified · found by the T08 browser flow · severity medium · page: help
- **Problem**: A preview opened by keyboard focus (a tooltip the pointer did not open) took the pointer's clicks as a hover preview does. Drawn over a neighbouring control, it took the first click meant for that control, and that click only closed it.
- **At 71587aa**: Fine-tune at 1850×960: Epochs typed, Tab. Focus is on the Seed field's ⓘ and its preview is at x 1063-1383, y 271-453, over the Graph backbone radios; `elementFromPoint` at "Frozen" is inside `#shep-help-bubble`. The first mouse click on Frozen only closed the preview (the radio stayed "Trained"); a second click chose it. An operator who does not notice starts a full fine-tune where they meant the frozen one. Evidence: scratchpad/t08/B/logs/b3-p_bubble2.out, b3-p_bubble.out, b3-s3_finetune-attempt1-bubble-blocked.out; scratchpad/t08/B/shots/b3-p3-bubble-covers-frozen-*.png, b3-p1-bubble-after-epochs-tab-w.png.
- **Done when**: A browser test at 1850×960 and a narrow size: Tab onto an ⓘ whose preview covers another control, then one click on that control reaches it (its state changes), and the preview is gone; it fails before.
- **Sources**: the T08 browser flow (scratchpad/t08/B/logs/b3-p_bubble2.out, scratchpad/t08/B/shots/b3-p3-bubble-covers-frozen-w.png); SK:113 (§7: hover and focus preview it, pinned it is a dialog); WCAG 1.4.13
- **Closed at**: 9c4580f. Root: a preview the pointer did not open took the pointer's events as one it opened does. Fix in the one shared bubble (help.py): it knows who opened it. A preview focus opened lets the reader's pointer through once the pointer moves over it (its pointer events off from that first move), so the click reaches the control under it; only its focus keeps or ends it, and the pointer leaving no longer closes it while its ⓘ has focus. A hover preview, or one whose ⓘ the pointer comes to, stays the pointer's (hoverable, WCAG 1.4.13); a pinned bubble is a dialog. The yield waits for the pointer's own move, as 5dd770e's rule does, so the shared walk's check that a focus preview never covers its own ⓘ (`focusWalk`, `elementsFromPoint`) still sees it and `test_a_focus_preview_closes_though_it_slid_under_a_resting_pointer` passes unchanged. Left as it is: a click with no pointer move at all, on a preview opened under a pointer already resting there, still lands on the preview (which then closes). Test: `test_webui_help.py::test_a_focus_preview_lets_the_click_through_to_the_control_under_it` (browser, paused page clock, 1850×960 and 420×800: one click on the covered radio chooses it and the preview closes; the pointer crossing a focus preview leaves it open while its focus holds). It fails on e0e4237 (the radio stays Trained). Screens (scratchpad/t08fix/U1/shots/), the served dashboard in operator mode: before/after_flow1-preview_1850x960.png and before/after_flow1-after-one-click_1850x960.png (one click on Frozen leaves Trained before, chooses Frozen after); at 960×1080 the preview covers no radio in this layout (before/after_flow1-preview_960x1080.png).
- **Re-checked (review of 9c4580f, 6da97da)**: with its pointer events off, a preview that had yielded passed the wheel to the page too. Where its height is capped (no side fits, its text scrolls inside), a wheel over it scrolled the page and not its text (420×360: the text at 0, the page 520 → 640), so the rest of the text could not be reached with the mouse, and the scroll moved the bubble with its ⓘ. Fix in the one bubble (`help.py`): while a preview has yielded, a wheel over its box scrolls its text while the text can move that way, then the page past the text's end, as a bubble that takes the pointer does; clicks still go through. The wheel listener is on the page only while a preview has yielded. Test: `test_webui_help.py::test_the_wheel_over_a_focus_preview_scrolls_its_text_though_clicks_go_through` (browser, 1850×960 and 420×360, a field preview taller than the window: the pointer over it goes through, the wheel moves its text and not the page, to its end, then the page); fails on 4b5cee6 (the page scrolled, the text at 0) and on e0e4237 (the preview took the pointer). No visible change; the review's probe: scratchpad/t08fix/review-ui/scripts/capped_probe.cjs.

#### B40 · Investigation notes shown only on Test, not on Models or loaded-model route

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · page: models
- **Problem**: Contested records on Models lack their notes.
- **At ba6e85e**: Investigation notes are read only by the Test page (test_page.py:670-684, read_investigation_notes). Models' contest_html (models_page.py:736-778) shows 'runs under these conditions differ — investigate…' with no note. Nothing under src/api reads notes for /api/v1/evaluations/loaded-model (grep finds no caller). The demo has no contested group, so this was not seen on screen.
- **Done when**: A contested record on Models and in /api/v1/evaluations/loaded-model shows its investigation notes, or 'notes cannot be read'.
- **Sources**: BL:998 (item 40); BL:1382
- **Closed at**: a3f9fa8. A record's condition group shows its investigation notes on Models, under the group's state, by the one rendering the Test page uses (`renderers.investigation_notes_html`: who, when, what, escaped; "cannot all be read" with its reason redacted, never "no note is kept"; "No investigation note is kept for this group." for a group whose runs differ); `record_states.contest_states` reads each version store's notes once, beside its scan, and the loaded-model route answers them (`contest.notes`, `contest.notes_readable`; no absolute path in a note's words), which the read-only view draws. #### R-68 · Empty recorded-results state is a dead end naming a file; segments show '· 0' twice

- **State**: partly done · found by the 2026-10-25 review · severity medium · page: models
- **Problem**: Models' empty recorded-results state was a dead end naming a file.
- **At ba6e85e**: Selecting a checkpoint with no ledger shows 'No ledger (evaluations.json) is beside this checkpoint, so no recorded result can be shown; that does not mean these weights were never evaluated.' (models_page.py:1437-1440). With a ledger that holds nothing for the file it shows 'This ledger holds no result for these weights; …' (RECORD_ABSENCE). Neither has a title, a next step or a 'Test this model ›' action. The segments 'Recorded results · 0 | Usage history · 0 | Results' still show with both counts at 0 (models_page.py:1413-1419). The table cell says 'no ledger' or 'none in ledger' (renderers.py:694-700).
- **Done when**: Each empty cause shows a titled state with one next step ('Test this model ›' or the read-only refusal), segments are hidden when both counts are 0, and no text says 'ledger'.
- **Sources**: UX:270 (appendix 68); RJ audits[2].findings[13]; UX:129
- **Closed at**: a3f9fa8. Each empty cause is a titled state with what it means and one next step, naming no file and never "ledger" (`models_page.records_empty_html`): "No recorded result yet" (none registered beside the checkpoint) and "No recorded result for these weights" (the results beside it are of other weights), each saying this does not mean the weights were never evaluated, then "Next: on the Test page, choose this model and start a test with Register the result on; its result then appears here." (the read-only view: recording a result is an operator control); a ledger that cannot be read says so with ▲ and its reason. The segments show no count of 0 ("Recorded results | Usage history | Results"; "Recorded results · 0 | Usage history · 0" before). Test: `test_webui_models_page.py::test_the_ledgers_states_are_said_and_name_no_directory` (fails on 97c8ec8). Seen at 1850x960 and 960x1080 on a probe-rc-disease file whose weights have no record beside it ("No recorded result for these weights … Next: on the Test page …"; "This ledger holds no result for these weights; …" before) (`scratchpad/t06c/models-records/shots/*/*_models_empty_*.png`). **Left, against Done when**: the next step is written words, not a "Test this model ›" action (no page links to another page with a model chosen today; that would be a new mechanism), and the segments are kept, without counts, because Results (the family's or workspace's results) stays reachable from a checkpoint with no record. Owner or review to decide whether either is still wanted.

#### R-82 · Same report record rendered by two code paths on Test and Models

- **State**: code-verified · found by the 2026-10-25 review · severity low · page: models
- **Problem**: Test and Models rendered the same record two ways.
- **At ba6e85e**: Some pieces are shared now: the one reader (record_views.result_view), relation rows (renderers.relation_rows :1810) and the R10 text (renderers.r10_rows :1867). The record itself is still rendered by two code paths: Test's _record_html (test_page.py:2094-2230) and Models' _mode_table (models_page.py:888-966). They show the same record in different forms and orders. Test gives task rows ('Pooled top-k k=3 0.3750, …', 'Macro recall@k …'), a Coverage row, an 'R10, training' key, and Environment, Native build, Dataset and Registration as separate rows. Models gives a table row, then one line chaining 24 metrics with ' · ' ('Disease coverage 0.8000 · Disease conservative pooled MRR 0.3250 · … · Disease macro precision@100 0.0100'), a bold 'R10, training inputs (evidence found): …' line, and 'Environment: … · Dataset: synthetic · Registered #2 at … from run …, report …'.
- **Done when**: One shared renderer produces the record's rows, and a unit test shows Test and Models emit the same rows in the same order, with Models' metric and Environment chains as rows.
- **Sources**: UX:284 (appendix 82); RJ audits[2].findings[18]; UX:127
- **Closed at**: a3f9fa8. `renderers.record_html` is the one rendering of a recorded result: the Test page's kept report (`test_page._record_html`) and each of Models' ledger records (`models_page.record_block_html`) draw it; a task's figures as tiles, then one summary list in one order (`renderers.record_rows`: Coverage, the figures by mode, Score semantics, Relations, R10 or the kind's lines, the kind's counts, Environment, Device, Deterministic algorithms, Native build, Dataset where not said by relation, Registration), every metric as recorded one click away. Models' records table, its column heads and record cell, and both pages' own coverage, counts, R10, metric, semantics, environment and native-build helpers are removed, with Test's and Models' R10 help topics (one `record-r10`). Test: `tests/unit/test_webui_models_page.py::test_test_and_models_draw_one_record_by_one_rendering_with_no_chain` (the same rows in the same order with the same values but Dataset and Registration, which only each page knows; no value with two " · "); fails on a git archive of 97c8ec8 with the tests copied in (14 of this part's new or changed tests fail there). Seen on a CPU service of the worktree (scratch HOME/XDG, M1, port 9038; the probe-rc workspace: six runs with ledgers on supplied, KG edge set and generated versions, one investigation note and one corrupt.pt added) against the same data served by a copy of 97c8ec8 (port 9037): Models › Recorded results at 1850x960 and 960x1080 (`scratchpad/t06c/models-records/shots/before/before_models_records_*.png`, `scratchpad/t06c/models-records/shots/after/after_models_records_*.png`), the Test page's KG link-prediction result (`*_test_result_probe-kg-test-1_*.png`); the records' text has no line with two " · " (2 such lines before). The page contract was broken by this fix and is whole again in 5ceb1ae: Models' notes problems imported `path_roots` and `redact` from the Test page, which a page may not reach (`tests/unit/test_webui_datasets.py::test_no_page_reaches_into_another_page_or_the_renderers_private_names` failed from a3f9fa8); the pair now lives in the shared `workspaces.py` (`path_roots`, `redact_server`) and both pages read it there.

#### B72-N4 · Option middle cut can split a multi-code-point emoji

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · severity minor · page: shell
- **Problem**: renderers.middle_cut can cut a multi-code-point emoji.
- **At ba6e85e**: middle_cut still slices by code point. Reproduced: a name holding the ZWJ family emoji (MAN, ZWJ, WOMAN, ZWJ, GIRL) cut to 20 characters keeps a lone GIRL after the ellipsis, and fit_option gives 'run-👨‍👩…👩‍👧-end · …', which splits the sequence.
- **Done when**: renderers.middle_cut and fit_option cut at grapheme-cluster boundaries; a unit test with a ZWJ family emoji name keeps the sequence whole or drops it entirely.
- **Sources**: BL:1030 (B0c review note)
- **Closed at**: 31ccf6e. `renderers.grapheme_ends` gives the offsets between grapheme clusters by the UAX #29 rules a name can meet (CR LF; a mark, variation selector, skin tone or tag character with what it follows; a Hangul syllable block; flags as pairs of regional indicators; nothing after a zero-width joiner), from `unicodedata`: the standard library has no grapheme segmentation (no UAX #29 break property, no Extended_Pictographic), so no dependency was added; it never cuts where UAX #29 would not and may keep a rarer sequence together. `middle_cut` (so `fit_option`, `fit_apart`, `fit_options` and the architecture cut) and `short_reason` cut only there, the end given the room the start could not use; the GPU chip's command line now goes through `short_reason` instead of its own copy of the cut. The row's case cut to 20 characters reads "run-👨‍👩‍👧…x👨‍👩‍👧-end"; before, every room from 10 to 18 characters left a part of the sequence (a lone GIRL, a ZWJ) beside the "…". Test: tests/unit/test_webui_renderers.py::test_a_cut_never_splits_a_character_drawn_from_several_code_points (rooms 1 to the name's length, four head lengths, `fit_option` with and without a mark, `fit_options`, `short_reason`; fails on 5137694 at (6, 6, 'run-👨…')). Not seen on a screen: the demo has no such name, so no screenshot. Not changed, recorded: Diagnosis' cohort options (`diagnosis_panel._option_text`) have a cutter of their own that still slices by code point, in the file the shared model list's lane changes.

It fails on c1e57c6 (1115 problems: both captions on the tabs' line at all 30 research-page stops, sizes and weights off the scale, borders under 3:1, three label styles, upper case, the browser's focus ring) and on 3a1a36f (202: the browser's focus ring, Top-K and Explanations under their own bubble, the run bar's fingerprint ⓘ under its bubble when stacked), and passes at a569948. The colour is rgb(122,70,0) on rgb(243,244,247), 7.06:1, from the token; there is no emoji and no raw hex. The versions are behind a closed native 'Versions ›' disclosure (7.39:1). Opened, it shows 'torch 2.10.0+cu130 · CUDA 13.0 (no device) · PyG 2.8.1 · native ext OK · Voyager'. A healthy runtime reads 'Runtime: every checked module imports.' with no mark. The row is 32 px high at 1850 and 960 wide.
- **Sources**: UX:221 (appendix 19); RJ audits[0].findings[5]; UX:252 (appendix 50); RJ audits[1].findings[15]; UX:120; BL:1030 (item 72 Shell); SK:107-108
- **Closed at**: —

#### V-1 · A Details the reader opened closes when their own act draws its region again

- **State**: code-verified · found by the verification of fcc3dea · severity medium · page: shell
- **Problem**: The disclosure keeper (T08-flow-2) kept a disclosure open only in the five regions a poll, a tick or a render draws again. A region the reader's own act draws again closed what the reader had opened.
- **At fcc3dea**: On the Training page, with four settings set the "Set by you" line counts one more behind its "Details ›"; opened, it closed when the operator set a fifth field. The fix lane's report on T08-flow-2 listed the same for the Fine-tune line, the Continue drawer's preview on a device or branch edit, and Models' details and records after a Load or an Order or Task change. Of the 133 `gr.HTML` outputs of an event in the built dashboard, 128 had no keeper.
- **Done when**: Every `gr.HTML` that an event draws runs the one keeper, listed from the built app by a test that fails on fcc3dea; a browser test shows a Details opened by the reader still open after their own edit draws it again.
- **Sources**: the fix lane's report on T08-flow-2 ("Seen, not fixed"); the verification brief, item 3
- **Closed at**: 4c72a2d. Root: the keeper was wired region by region, while what an HTML holds is its renderers' choice at run time. Fix: `shell.plain_html`, the dashboard's one HTML constructor, runs `renderers.KEEP_OPEN_JS` before any script of its own, and the chart figure does the same; the per-page wiring it replaces is gone (Training's `_POPOVER_JS` prefix, the Test job card and result, the Diagnosis card). Tests: `test_webui_shell.py::test_every_html_an_event_draws_keeps_its_disclosures_as_the_reader_left_them` (all 133, the keeper once and first; fails on fcc3dea) and `test_training_page.py::test_a_details_the_reader_opened_stays_open_when_their_own_edit_draws_it_again` (browser, 1850×960: open across the next field set, closed by the reader stays closed; fails on fcc3dea). The shared UI walk passes. No visible change.
- **Re-checked (review of 5033d29, 2026-11-17)**: with the keeper on every HTML, a region that shows one of many and named nothing kept a reader's open Details for the next thing shown: Models' "Details ›" opened for one checkpoint was drawn open for the next one selected (open at 1850×960 and 390×844; closed at fcc3dea, where every drawing closed it), and the same held for a record's folds, a Diagnosis candidate's "More paths" and "Written summary", a workspace's manifest and versions on Datasets, the Continue drawer's settings and a Results row's or Compare's folds. Fixed at 126c6e1: each such region's renderer names the thing shown (`renderers.keep_as`; `details_list(of=…)`): the checkpoint's key, each record's registration and report, the candidate, the workspace, the checkpoint checked (its key in the list or its path as shown, never the server's path, and its fingerprint), each result row and the results compared. Test: `test_webui_shell.py::test_a_region_that_can_show_another_thing_names_what_its_disclosures_are_of` (fails at 5033d29 and fcc3dea). Seen with the real keeper on Models' identity region: A opened, A drawn again open, B drawn closed; at 5033d29 B open; at fcc3dea A drawn again closed (scratchpad/verify/fix/vr/r2/keep_after.json, keep_before.json, keep_old.json; shots/keep_*_1850x960.png and _390x844.png). The shared UI walk passes.

#### V-2 · A status mark's text reads its shape and word with no space between

- **State**: code-verified · found by the verification of fcc3dea · severity low · page: shell
- **Problem**: A status mark's hidden shape was followed directly by its word, so copied text and `innerText` read "○Saved" and "✕Not usable"; a mark drawn as a chip read the shape and the word as two lines.
- **At fcc3dea**: `renderers.status_mark` drew `<span class="shep-st-shape" aria-hidden="true">○</span><span class="shep-st-w">Saved</span>`; in Chromium with the shell's style sheet, `innerText` and a copy read "●Saved" (text) and "●\nSaved" (chip). The config lane's report on T08-flow-10 saw it in Settings ("○Saved. …").
- **Done when**: Every mark, as text and as a chip, reads shape, space, word when copied and as `innerText`; a screen reader still reads only the word; the look is unchanged, shown by screens before and after at 1850×960 and a narrow size.
- **Sources**: the config lane's report on T08-flow-10 ("Seen, not changed here"); the verification brief, item 4
- **Closed at**: 29a4605. Root: no text between the shape and the word, and a chip's shape and word were separate flex items. Fix in the one renderer: a space of no width between them (`renderers.MARK_SPACE`; the shape's 4 px margin still draws the gap), a chip's shape, space and word one inline run (`shep-st-in`), none after the spinner; the touch script's "not sent" mark takes the same space. Test: `test_webui_renderers.py::test_a_status_marks_text_reads_shape_space_word_and_its_look_is_kept` (browser: every mark, text and chip, 14 and 15 px, at 1850×960 and 390×844: "● Saved" copied and as `innerText`, the shape aria-hidden, the gap 4 px, 6 px after the spinner; fails on fcc3dea). Every mark's gap and width measured the same before and after. Screens: scratchpad/verify/fix/item4/shots/before_status-marks_1850x960.png, _390x844.png and after_status-marks_1850x960.png, _390x844.png.
- **Re-checked (verification fix stage)**: the test measured the spinner's gap from its turning box (a turned 12 px square is up to 17 px wide), so it read 5.12 px once where 6 px is drawn. 0ed6104: the test's browser asks for reduced motion, under which the style sheet holds the spinner still; the same gap is measured. No product change.

#### V-6 · A reloaded research page can stop following its job: the session's location ends on Diagnosis

- **State**: code-verified · found by the verification of fcc3dea · severity high · page: shell
- **Problem**: After a reload of Research › Training (or Test, or Back then Forward), the session's location could end on Diagnosis while Training was shown, so every Training poll idled: the pill stayed on "RUNNING" for a finished run and Start stayed disabled until the reader clicked another tab and back.
- **At fcc3dea**: Gradio sends a tab's select when the tab mounts; Diagnosis' mounts late and its select was sent after the restore's Research and Training selects. `LOCATION_WRITES` orders the writers by arrival, so it wrote "clinical" last (7 of 24 Training loads, 4 of 12 Test loads; scratchpad/verify/rb1/run2/json/t1h-location.json, t1-train.json). B46-mount's recorded residual is the same case.
- **Done when**: Every location writer writes only for the page shown as it was sent and never over a later write; tests fail on fcc3dea; RB1's reload probe finds no load ending on Diagnosis.
- **Sources**: the verification of fcc3dea, part RB1 (V-RB1-1)
- **Closed at**: 9b66b1a. Each writer is given, by its js (`shell.SENT_JS`, `ShepShell.sent`), the page the browser shows as it is sent and when, and writes only for its own page and no earlier than the write applied last (`shell._written`, a written-at State in the same group). Tests: `test_webui_shell.py::test_a_location_writer_writes_only_for_the_page_shown_as_it_was_sent` and `::test_a_mount_time_select_that_arrives_late_never_moves_the_location` (browser: Diagnosis' mount-time select held until Models is answered); both fail on fcc3dea. RB1's probe (t1h_location, 18 loads of Training on the served dashboard): 6 ended on Diagnosis at fcc3dea, 0 after (scratchpad/verify/fix/r2/loc/json/t1h-before.json, t1h-after.json). `test_webui_shell.py`: 54 passed, the UI walk included.
- **Re-checked (review of 5033d29, 2026-11-17)**: t1h-after.json's header said "commit": "fcc3dea", its probe's fixed label; it measured the fix worktree at 02347b4 with the change committed as 9b66b1a (its after.log serves /home/user/shep-wt-ui). The file now says so (`commit`, `commit_note`); the numbers above stand.

**UI-16, test**

#### B72-C2 · Recorded LP metrics show raw names, unrounded values, no units

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · severity minor · page: test
- **Problem**: Test's and Models' recorded LP metrics shown raw.
- **At ba6e85e**: A KG link-prediction report shows 'Recorded metrics: lp_accuracy 0.3333333333333333 / lp_average_precision 0.4771524771524771 / lp_loss 1.0628392696380615 / lp_roc_auc 0.3888888888888889'. These are the raw names, unrounded, with no units, on both Test and Models. Word labels (LP_METRIC_WORDS, 'ROC-AUC' and so on) exist in caveats but this display does not use them.
- **Done when**: On Test and Models a KG link-prediction report shows plain metric names (e.g. 'ROC-AUC'), values rounded to 4 digits, and units stated once.
- **Sources**: BL:1030
- **Closed at**: a3f9fa8. A KG link-prediction record's metrics are rows under their plain names (`caveats.LP_METRIC_WORDS`: "Accuracy (raw > 0)", "ROC-AUC", "Average precision", "Loss (the LP head's hinge)"), each to four decimals with its unit and denominator said once under it as the record's `measured` holds them (or "Not computable"); the values as recorded are one click away ("Recorded metrics (4)") and every export keeps them. Test: `test_webui_test_page.py::test_a_kg_link_prediction_records_metrics_read_in_plain_names_on_test_and_models` (Test and Models draw the same rows; fails on 97c8ec8). Seen on the Test page's probe-kg-test-1 result at 1850x960 and 960x1080: "ROC-AUC 0.4722 probability that a positive's raw score is above a negative's … over 36 (positive, negative) raw-score comparisons over the whole set" where it read "lp_roc_auc 0.4722222222222222" (`scratchpad/t06c/models-records/shots/*/*_test_result_probe-kg-test-1_*.png`, `*_test_probe-kg-test-1_*.txt`). Models by the unit test (its LP record not opened in the browser).
- **Re-checked (T08 flow, 71587aa)**: 2026-11-17, its Done when was broken on Models › Results: the KG link prediction table under the Disease table had one "Recorded metrics" column ("lp_accuracy 0.4166666666666667 lp_average_precision 0.514021164021164 …") where Test and the Models detail read "Accuracy (raw > 0) 0.4167", "ROC-AUC 0.4722" (scratchpad/t08/B/json/flow.json p_lp_table, scratchpad/t08/B/logs/b6-p_lp_table.out, scratchpad/t08/B/shots/b6-03-results-lp-table-*.png). Root: the Results segment drew metrics in columns only for `models_page.RECORD_COLUMNS` (the described tasks) and every other protocol's as recorded, beside the record rendering of R-82, which kept the LP words and rounding to itself (`renderers._lp_rows`). Fixed at 8558074: the rendering's parts are shared (`renderers.lp_metric_names`, `lp_metric_words`, `lp_value_html`) and the Results table reads them: a column per LP metric under its plain name, four decimals, "Not computable" where the record could not compute one, a value its condition group's runs differ on unrounded (as for tasks), each metric's unit said once under the table. Compare keeps the values as recorded, with their unit and denominator rows; every export keeps them. Test: `test_models_results_segment.py::test_a_kg_link_prediction_table_shows_plain_names_four_decimals_and_units_once` (fails on e0e4237). Seen in the real app on a copy of the T08 workspace: heads "Accuracy (raw > 0) · ROC-AUC · Average precision · Loss (the LP head's hinge)", the b-ft-full-disease row "0.4167 0.4722 0.5140 1.3817", "Units" under the table (scratchpad/t08fix/U3/json/after-look.json). Screens: scratchpad/t08fix/U3/shots/before-results-lp-w.png and after-results-lp-w.png (1850×960), the -n pair at 960×1080. State kept code-verified.

#### B72-T1 · Measurement refusals name the protocol by its code

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · page: test
- **Problem**: protocols.inputs_refusal names the protocol by code (pinned by the P0-2 golden).
- **At ba6e85e**: The measurement refusals still name the protocol by its code. Rendered at ba6e85e: '…which the patients_like_me measurement needs. Import a source that lists causal_genes.'; '…which the kg_link_prediction measurement scores…'; 'the kg_link_prediction measurement does not measure a supplied cohort version'. model_refusal uses the same '{protocol.name}' form. A helper _named already gives plain words with the code beside, but only available()'s reason uses it. Start shows the sentence verbatim. Not seen live: the demo's models and versions do not trigger it.
- **Done when**: Rendered measurement and model refusals name the measurement by its plain label (code only beside it), with the P0-2 golden updated to match.
- **Sources**: BL:1030
- **Closed at**: df9e8f9. `protocols._said` names a measurement and a task head by their words in every refusal ("the patients-like-me measurement", "the KG link prediction measurement", "the patients-like-me head"; a list "disease or KG link prediction"), and `_ref` ends the sentence with the names whose words differ (" Ref: patients_like_me, kg_link_prediction."): `model_refusal`, `inputs_refusal`, `available` (whose "KG link prediction (kg_link_prediction)" went too), the fixture gaps and the unknown-name sentence. One source, so Start, the job runner, the command line and the probe say it alike. The P0-2 golden regenerated by that name (its test's docstring names the change); the test-only protocols p1 and p2 carry no words. Tests: `test_golden_p0_2_protocols.py`, `test_protocols.py`, `test_webui_test_page.py::test_measure_refusal_says_why_each_kind_is_not_measured`, `::test_a_measurement_needs_a_version_that_lists_its_genes` and the pins in test_job_runner, test_model_capabilities, test_kg_link_prediction (fail on 6db401b). Not seen live: the demo's models and versions do not trigger a refusal; Start's reason is the pinned string.

#### NEW-test-5 · Result calls a report 'the selected workspace' on equal dataset digest only

- **State**: code-verified · found by the 2026-11-16 audit · page: test
- **Duplicate of**: R-64 (closed with it)
- **At ba6e85e**: Inferred, not seen: the Result says 'the selected workspace' whenever the report's dataset record digest equals the selected workspace's (test_page.py:1909-1911). So a report measured in another workspace built on the same dataset record would be attributed to the selected one.
- **Done when**: A unit test with a report measured in another workspace on the same dataset record shows the Result naming that other workspace, not 'the selected workspace'.
- **Sources**: found by the 2026-11-16 audit at ba6e85e
- **Closed at**: f6b9289. A report records the dataset record it measured and never the workspace it read (records carry no path), so the Result's identity row is "Dataset" and says the relation the record supports: "the selected workspace's dataset record", every other listed workspace holding the same record named under it ("demo holds the same record; the report does not record which workspace was read"); another record by the workspaces holding it and its origin (the one reader's view). Test: `test_a_result_is_the_selected_workspaces_only_by_the_relation_its_record_holds` (measured in demo, demo-copy on the same record selected: demo is named; fails on 6db401b). Seen on probe-rc, whose rules1-demo holds the same dataset record: "the selected workspace's dataset record / rules1-demo holds the same record; …" (`scratchpad/t06c/test-page/shots/after/after_result_1850x960.png`). Reading of the Done when: the other workspace is named as a holder of the record, since which one was read is not recorded; "the selected workspace" alone is never said of a result.

#### RJ-61b · Test's model choice gives its instruction twice and preselects nothing

- **State**: code-verified · found by the 2026-10-25 review · severity high (finding 61 as a whole); this part low · page: test
- **Problem**: Review finding 61 also recorded that 'Choose the model to test.' and 'Disabled: choose a model.' give the same instruction twice, and that the Model select had nothing preselected. Its fix: drop the first line, preselect 'best' (ML-CK-3). At ba6e85e model_html still says 'Choose the run, then the checkpoint to test.' (test_page.py:1485) while start_state says 'Disabled: choose a model.' (:1697) once a version is chosen. That is inferred from the code: the demo has no data version, so Start's reason read 'Disabled: choose a data version.' (c1.json). Nothing is preselected (Run empty, c1.json). That is deliberate: model_list.py:399-402 says the Test page 'never measures a model it picked itself'.

#### R-72+B72 · Relation rows still lead with their code (R1, R2...) on Test and Models

- **State**: code-verified · found by the 2026-10-25 review · severity medium · page: test
- **Problem**: Test's version details and Relations, and Models' 'Relations:' line (~30 ' · ' facts), were run-on dot strings.
- **At ba6e85e**: The run-on chains are gone: the cohort version is a summary list, and each relation is its own row with a shared mark. The finding's title is '…run-on dot strings that lead with codes', and its fix put the meaning first with the code after ('Graph files match manifest (R1) ✓'). The relation rows still open with their codes: 'R1 graph files match the workspace manifest', 'R2 generated split's samples…', 'R3 model trained on this graph', 'R5 dataset record bound to this graph'. This is by design in the one renderer (renderers.py:1828-1829: label = f"{name} {display}"), on Test and on Models. See own-r1-unreg-after.txt lines 135-141 and p-models-row0.txt in the checkers' out directories. That breaks skill §5's front-loading: the first two words carry the code. The same Models record block still runs a 24-metric ' · ' chain and an 'Environment: … · Dataset: … · Registered …' chain (p-models-row0.txt lines 59-60). That part belongs to R-82's partly verdict. This is a minor residual, but it is the substance the title names.
- **Done when**: Each relation row on Test and Models starts with its meaning and shows the code after it (e.g. 'Graph files match manifest (R1)'), and its key does not wrap to 3 lines.
- **Fix**: `renderers.relation_rows` puts the meaning first and the code after ('Graph files match the workspace manifest (R1)', 'Model trained on this graph (R3)'; a relation without words its recorded name, 'Data version bound to this graph' as before), and `renderers.relations_html` is their one list on Test (under one 'Relations' key across the Result) and Models (under each record), its key column holding the whole name on one line. Models' 24-metric chain and its Environment line stay R-82's.
- **Sources**: UX:274 (appendix 72); RJ audits[2].findings[8]; BL:1030 (item 72 Models)
- **Closed at**: 751b8d7. Tests: `test_webui_renderers.py::test_the_research_table_row_has_its_columns_and_escapes`, `test_webui_models_page.py::test_the_detail_keeps_validation_and_mode_c_apart`, `test_webui_test_page.py::test_the_result_shows_aggregates_and_states_never_a_case_or_a_path` (fail on 0f4ae91: 'R3 model trained …'). #### T08-flow-11 · A Test run on a host that cannot give the device is started, then fails with "Process exited with code 1"

- **State**: code-verified · found by the T08 browser flow · page: test
- **Problem**: On a CPU host with no SHEPHERD_DEVICE, Test's Start was allowed; the run was spawned and failed, and the card's lead said only "No report kept · Process exited with code 1"; the device reason ("--device auto requires CUDA …", `scripts/measure_scorer.py` ~1057) was only in the run output. Training, Continue, Branch, Fine-tune and the previews refuse such a device before anything is spawned, in words.
- **At 71587aa**: `TrainingManager.start_test` (`src/api/services/training_manager.py` ~1700-1745) took the deployment's device (`deployment_device()`; None left measure_scorer's `auto`) and spawned without the check every other start makes (`_training_device`, `resolve_training_device`). Seen in scratchpad/t08/B/json/flow.json (`s4_measure_no_device.lp-base-nodevice.job`, `p_testdetails_nodevice`), B/logs/b5-s4_measure_no_device.out and B/logs/b5-p_testdetails_nodevice.out.
- **Done when**: A runner test on a host without CUDA and no device set finds the refusal before anything is spawned, in a request's words that say what the operator can do from the Test page (which has no Device field), and a test with `cpu` set still starts; it fails on e0e4237.
- **Fix**: `start_test` resolves the deployment's device by the one rule (`_test_device_refusal`: `resolve_training_device`, as each training start asks it) after the protocol is judged and before the slot is taken, a job directory made, anything spawned or the GPU lock taken. It refuses in `TEST_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE`'s words: the device is the service's, and on a development host the operator stops the service (Settings › Stop service) and starts it with the CPU device; the variable only in the trailing Ref. Its callers: the Test page (`start_test_run`, whose outcome line shows the words) and the deployment probe, whose in-process starts all run under its settings with SHEPHERD_DEVICE set; no HTTP route starts a test run. The tests that start a test job on a CPU host now set the device they mean (`cpu`): test_job_runner.py's autouse fixture, test_protocols.py's runner fixture, test_backend_shutdown.py's closed-slot test and test_probe_m5_acceptance.py's held fixture; each still checks what it checked. On GB10 nothing changes: CUDA is there.
- **Sources**: the T08 browser flow (scratchpad/t08/B/json/flow.json, B/logs/b5-s4_measure_no_device.out, B/logs/b5-p_testdetails_nodevice.out)
- **Closed at**: 4d485c7. Test: `test_job_runner.py::test_a_test_run_on_a_device_this_host_cannot_give_is_refused_before_anything` (no device, `auto`, `cuda`, on a host without CUDA: the refusal in those words, nothing spawned, no job directory, no lock holder; with `cpu` set it starts with `--device cpu`): 3 cases fail on e0e4237. Related files passed, the two integration files that run real test jobs among them (test_job_runner_measure.py 7, test_test_page_job.py 2). Seen on dashboards started by the real launcher on a scratch site (the demo workspace, one imported cohort version, a one-epoch disease child from tests/fixtures), no SHEPHERD_DEVICE, the local start: on e0e4237 (port 9142) Start said "The test run started…" and the card ended "Failed · No report kept · Process exited with code 1"; on the worktree (port 9161) the line beside Start gave the refusal and the job line stayed "No test run since the server started." (scratchpad/t08fix/K/t5/shots/before_test-start-no-device_1850x960.png, _760x1000.png, after_test-start-no-device_1850x960.png, _760x1000.png; K/t5/json/). Not changed: Start is not disabled before a click; the Device row reads "auto (requires CUDA) · fixed by the server" and the refusal comes on Start.

#### V-18 · Three copies of "auto means CUDA"; measure_scorer takes cuda:N on a host without CUDA

- **State**: code-verified · found by the verification of fcc3dea, recorded by the review of 5033d29 · severity low · page: test
- **Problem**: The rule "auto means CUDA, refused without it" has three copies: `training_device` (training, the job runner, the Test start's check), `measure_scorer._resolve_device` and `pipeline.resolve_serving_device`. They differ on cuda:N: the measurement's copy refuses only auto and cuda, so `measure_scorer --device cuda:0` on a host without CUDA fails late, in torch.
- **At fcc3dea**: scripts/measure_scorer.py `_resolve_device` (~1032–1066, `requested == "cuda"` only); training's `wants_cuda` and serving refuse cuda:N too. The Test page is safe: its start's check (the job runner, 4d485c7) is stricter than the measurement it guards; only the command line fails late. Pre-existing copies. Evidence: scratchpad/verify/aud/shared_sources_fcc3dea.md §1 and §7.
- **Done when**: One device rule (training_device's) decides auto, cuda and cuda:N for training, measurement and serving, each refusing in its own words before anything is read; a test of `measure_scorer --device cuda:0` refused up front on a host without CUDA.
- **Sources**: the verification of fcc3dea, part AUD (V-AUD-6); the review of 5033d29 (R-4: the finding was in no row)
- **Corrected (verification, d8d1828, 2026-11-17)**: the Problem's last sentence is wrong: `measure_scorer --device cuda:0` does not fail late in torch. The parser's `--device` takes only auto, cuda and cpu, so `cuda:N` is refused before `_resolve_device` runs. Run at 8ddb05f on this host (no CUDA, no SHEPHERD_DEVICE): `python scripts/measure_scorer.py --device cuda:0` printed "measure_scorer.py: error: argument --device: invalid choice: 'cuda:0' (choose from 'auto', 'cuda', 'cpu')" and exited 2 (scratchpad/verify/fix2/v18/cuda0_8ddb05f.out). What stays true: three copies of the rule, two of them asking torch themselves; the copies differ on `cuda:N` only where the measurement's command line cannot take it. The Done when's `cuda:0` test is read as: the three decide by one rule, each refusing auto and cuda (training and serving also `cuda:N`) without CUDA.
- **Fixed (verification, 808ff97, 2026-11-17)**: measurement's `_resolve_device` and serving's `resolve_serving_device` ask the one rule (`training_device.wants_cuda`, `cuda_visible`) instead of torch; each keeps its own words and guards (serving's PyTorch check first, blank meaning auto). Nothing accepted or refused changed, nor any words or `cuda_executed`. The rule's module docstring names its three askers; ARCHITECTURE's device row names the rule. Test: `test_training_device.py::test_training_measurement_and_serving_decide_by_the_one_rule` (rule made False, then True, torch saying the opposite: each refuses or takes auto and cuda, training and serving also cuda:1); fails on d8d1828.
- **Closed at**: 808ff97. One device rule for training, measurement and serving (the Fixed line above).

**UI-16, training**

#### B41 · Chart is silent when a drawn run cannot get its newer epochs

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · page: training
- **Problem**: After 20 retries an ended run stays at older epochs with no message.
- **At ba6e85e**: From the code; the demo cannot make the series server function fail. After RETRY_MAX (20) failed retries, setMessage runs only when inst.run === null (shep_charts.js:819-827). A drawn run that cannot get its newer epochs therefore stays at its older epochs with no message.
- **Done when**: When the series function fails RETRY_MAX times for a drawn run, the chart shows a line over the curves such as 'Newer epochs could not be loaded.' (covered by a JS unit test).
- **Sources**: BL:999 (item 41); PTR:2380-2381
- **Closed at**: 40eff69. When the series function fails RETRY_MAX (20) times for the run drawn, its curves stay and the figure's toolbar shows the shared amber ▲ with "Newer epochs could not be loaded: reload the page to try again." (`charts.STALE`, a static note the module shows or hides; never over a plot); it goes once a reply for that run, another run or none is drawn. Test: the JS unit test `tests/fixtures/chart_host.cjs` scenario `gave_up_on_new_epochs_says_so_over_the_curves` (run by `test_webui_charts.py::test_a_reply_is_drawn_only_for_what_the_token_asks_for_now`; fails on aad658c: the note never shows), `test_webui_charts.py::test_the_toolbar_says_when_newer_epochs_could_not_be_loaded`. Seen on the CPU demo at 1850x960 with the series request refused by the browser (Playwright route) after a new epoch was logged: 21 failed calls, the 4 epochs kept, the note at y 271-299 above the plots; at aad658c the same 21 calls left nothing said. Screens: scratchpad/t06b/run-view/shots/before/b41_gave_up_1850x960.png, scratchpad/t06b/run-view/shots/after/b41_gave_up_1850x960.png (no narrow pair: the note is in the toolbar's own row, which wraps).

#### B69-T2 · Run with only best.pt reads 'dataset not recorded (no checkpoint)'

- **State**: code-verified · found by an internal review · severity cosmetic · page: training
- **Problem**: The header reads last.pt only.
- **At ba6e85e**: From the code; not seen, because the demo has no run with only best.pt. _snapshot_for reads checkpoints/last.pt only (training_console.py:2179-2195). With none, the header says 'dataset not recorded (no checkpoint)' (:2241-2243), and the state line also says 'No checkpoint saved, so nothing can be continued' (:2361-2362), even though best.pt exists and the drawer lists it.
- **Done when**: For a run with only best.pt, the run-view header names the dataset and the state line does not say no checkpoint was saved, verified by a unit test of _snapshot_for on such a run.
- **Sources**: BL:1027
- **Closed at**: 7795d37. The one reader of a run's latest state reads the file `checkpoint_paths.latest_saved` names: its `last.pt`, else its newest listed checkpoint file (`select_checkpoint_in_dir`'s fallback), or none; `TrainingManager.latest_snapshot` and the run view's `_snapshot_for` (its cache keyed on that file) both use it, so the stop's outcome says the same. Test: `test_training_page.py::test_a_run_that_kept_only_best_pt_names_its_dataset_and_its_state` (a real best.pt through `_snapshot_for`; fails on aad658c: no snapshot). Seen on the CPU demo (a base run's copy keeping only `best.pt`): before "dataset not recorded (no checkpoint)" and "No checkpoint saved, so nothing can be continued"; after "● same dataset as the run bar" and "Finished · 4 of 4 epochs completed. To train further, use Continue…". Screens: scratchpad/t06b/run-view/shots/before/bestonly_1850x960.png, scratchpad/t06b/run-view/shots/after/bestonly_1850x960.png (and _960x1080).
- **Re-checked (T06 review fix, 48a7ac3)**: the closing evidence used only a best.pt that records the run's last epoch. A best.pt saved mid-run records no end, so a 4-epoch run that kept only a best.pt of epoch 2 read '2 of 4 epochs completed · can be continued', as if the run had stopped there, while its charts showed 4. A state read from a file other than the run's `last.pt` is that file's, so the run view's line names it first (`run_snapshot_html`): 'best.pt: 2 of 4 epochs completed · can be continued' (and 'best.pt: Finished · 4 of 4 epochs completed…' for this row's case); the stop notice already names the file by its path (`_snapshot_sentence`). Test: `test_training_page.py::test_a_best_pt_saved_mid_run_is_said_as_that_files_state_not_the_runs` (a real best.pt through `_snapshot_for`; fails on 54d5f4b). Seen on the CPU demo (a run copy keeping only a best.pt rewritten to epoch 2 of 4 with no end, demo-midbest) at 1850x960 and 960x1080: before '2 of 4 epochs completed · can be continued'; after 'best.pt: 2 of 4 epochs completed · can be continued'. Screens: scratchpad/t06b/fixer/shots/before/midbest_1850x960.png, scratchpad/t06b/fixer/shots/after/midbest_1850x960.png (and _960x1080).

#### R-32 · Device default 'auto' is refused on a CUDA-less host with no warning before Start

- **State**: code-verified · found by the 2026-10-25 review · severity high · page: training
- **Problem**: The Device help said 'auto' falls back to CPU; the trainer refuses 'auto' without CUDA.
- **At ba6e85e**: The help text is fixed. Both Device help texts say auto is CUDA and is refused without it (training_console.py:4248-4254 for the base form, :4298-4305 for the Continue drawer; finetune_page.py:1131 the same). Since AF1 dc08792 the job runner resolves the device before it spawns anything (training_manager.py:619-632 and :1264; training_device.py:25-49), so Start with auto on this host is refused and creates no run (inferred from the code: Start was not pressed, per the audit rules). The B5 footer (6a9a7c3) leads with 'No CUDA device visible: GPU training is unavailable…'. What remains: Device still defaults to auto on Training (:4439-4440), in the Continue drawer (:4518-4519) and on Fine-tune (finetune_page.py:1249-1250). Start and Continue Training stay enabled, and nothing at the Device field or beside Start says auto will be refused here. Beside Start the reason line reads 'Stop disabled: no training is running.' In the Continue drawer, Check with the default auto answers in the CLI's words: '--device auto requires CUDA, which is not available here. … Pass --device cpu explicitly for development; the run records the device it was given. Nothing was started.' (seen)
- **Done when**: On a host with no CUDA device, the Device radios on Training, Continue and Fine-tune show an info line that auto and cuda will be refused (choose cpu), or a reason beside Start, and the refusal text uses page words.

#### R-42+owner · Training help texts lack bold leads, exceed budget, and Model-spec fields lack help

The epoch axis is labelled once per chart grid, under it, by the figure (`figure_payload`'s `x_label`, "Epoch (shared axis)"); the per-chart label and its CSS are gone; the grid keeps its rows' height so the label stays under the last row (6d8a46a: with the drawer open the rows had run over it). #### T08-flow-2 · Training's "Details ›" popovers close under the reader when the poll redraws

- **State**: code-verified · found by the T08 browser flow · severity medium · page: training
- **Problem**: The run view header's "Run details" popover and the run bar's dataset chip "Details ›" closed by themselves while read: each drawing a running job's render made of the header or the chip drew its popover closed again. No Escape, click outside or focus change came first.
- **At 71587aa**: During a CPU run, "Run details" (`#shep-train-run-details`) closed 0.5–1.5 s after it was opened, when the header was drawn again ("dataset recorded at the first checkpoint" became "● same dataset as the run bar"), at 1850×960 and 960×1080; the chip's popover (`#shep-train-details`) closed in the drawing that said "Completed". Evidence: scratchpad/t08/C/json/c8c-train-redraw.json, scratchpad/t08/C/json/c8c2-train-popover-shots.json, scratchpad/t08/C/shots/c8c2-*.png.
- **Done when**: A browser test drives a real redraw of the Training run header and chip (the poll, or the same server push) with a popover open and finds it still open with focus unchanged, and closed after Escape stays closed through the next redraw; the Test card's keep-open test still passes; it fails before.
- **Sources**: the T08 browser flow (scratchpad/t08/C/json/c8c2-train-popover-shots.json, scratchpad/t08/C/shots/c8c2-run-details-w-closed.png); B52-E (the Test job card's Details, kept for a `details` only by `renderers.KEEP_OPEN_JS`, 27637d5)
- **Closed at**: 8b75d85. Root: `training_console._POPOVER_JS` kept "open" only in the DOM (the popover's `hidden`, its button's `aria-expanded`) while the server's HTML draws it closed, and Gradio 6.30 draws a `gr.HTML` again in place, setting each element's attributes to the new HTML's. The keeper written for the Test card covered `details` only, keyed by its words and place (so it also kept a Details open for the next job). Fix: one shared keeper, `renderers.KEEP_OPEN_JS`, for both kinds (a `details`; a button with `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls` and its popover in the same HTML). It tells the reader's own change (a click, Enter or Space, Escape, a click outside, focus leaving) from a drawing's by a mark it sets and a drawing takes off, and puts the reader's last choice back after each drawing without moving focus. Where a drawing replaced the control the reader's focus was on (the chip's "in use by the running job" coming and going shifts its popover), it puts focus back on the same place, without scrolling, so Escape still closes it. A disclosure is the same one while its region names the same thing (`renderers.keep_as`): the chip its workspace, the header its run, the Test card its job, the Test result its report, the Diagnosis card's Details its state and time; a choice for one no longer drawn is dropped. Wired to every `gr.HTML` a poll, tick or render draws again that holds a disclosure: Training's chip and run header (`_POPOVER_JS` starts with it), the Test job card, the Test result (drawn again when the poll sees a job start or end) and the Diagnosis card (its not-serving reason's Details; the slow poll). Checked and not wired: the Fine-tune job card (no disclosure); Training's error banner (its own `_BANNER_JS`, a size default and a choice for the page; one failure's HTML does not change); Models' detail and records, Datasets, Diagnosis' candidate Details, Results, Settings' data locations, the footer, the "Set by you" lines and the Continue drawer, drawn again only by the reader's own act, or once. Tests: `test_training_page.py::test_the_details_popovers_stay_as_the_reader_left_them_across_redraws` (browser; both popovers drawn by their renderers with the page's own script and pushed as a running job's render does, at 1850×960 and 960×1080: open through two drawings with focus on its ×, closed by Escape or a click outside and still closed after the next drawing, drawn closed for another run; fails on e0e4237, where the first drawing closed it); `test_webui_test_page.py::test_the_job_cards_details_stay_as_the_reader_left_them_while_the_run_prints` (passes, and now also finds the next job's card drawn closed, which e0e4237 left open). Seen in the real app, a 12-epoch CPU run on the demo workspace (port 9141, scratch HOME/XDG): "Run details" open through every header drawing (epochs 1-3, the dataset relation's change) with focus on its ×, closed after Escape through the next drawings; the chip's details open through the "Completed" drawing with focus on its × (scratchpad/t08fix/U2/json/u2-popover-look.json). Screens: scratchpad/t08fix/U2/shots/after-run-details-kept-w.png, after-dataset-details-after-end-w.png (and -n, 960×1080); before, at 71587aa: scratchpad/t08/C/shots/c8c2-run-details-w-closed.png, c8c2-ds-details-w-closed.png (and -n).
- **Re-checked (review of 8b75d85, f691dd8)**: the keeper put focus back where the reader had left it. Its record of the control last focused in a disclosure was dropped only on a focusout with a relatedTarget. After Escape (focus on the chip's Details button) and a click on page text (focus to the page, no relatedTarget), the record stayed, and the next drawing that replaced the button focused it again: the chip's "in use by the running job" lead goes and comes at a job's start and end. Space then opened the dataset popover; a stale chip record also pulled focus from the run header (the review's probe: scratchpad/t08fix/review-ui/logs/probe_focus_head.json). Root: Chromium sends the same focusout (no relatedTarget, the control still in the page) for a drawing that removes the focused control and for a click on nothing focusable (scratchpad/t08fix/fix-ui/scripts/focus_events.cjs). Fix in the one keeper (`renderers.KEEP_OPEN_JS`): on such a focusout the place is forgotten once the script running now is done (a microtask, after a drawing's removal) if the control is still in the page and focus is not on it; a control the drawing removed keeps its place. It serves every region wired to it: Training's chip and run header, the Test job card and result, the Diagnosis card. Test: the same browser test now also clicks the page after Escape, pushes two drawings and finds focus on the page and Space opening nothing, and checks that focus on the chip's button, which the drawing after Escape replaces, moves to the new button; it fails on 4b5cee6 (focus back on the chip's details) and on e0e4237. No visible change; no screens.

#### V-5 · The run bar's reason runs over Start and Continue… in a narrow window

- **State**: code-verified · found by the T08 fix lane, recorded by the verification of fcc3dea · severity medium · page: training
- **Problem**: A long run bar reason (the GPU lock's refusal with no workspaces folder set) ran over the run bar's buttons, so a click on Start reached the reason's text.
- **At fcc3dea**: For an operator with no workspaces folder set, at 960×1080 the reason's line spanned x 358–661 while its slot was x 358–518, over Start (x 532–609); `elementFromPoint` at Start's centre returned the reason's SPAN; the same at 390×844. Evidence: scratchpad/t08fix/U1/shots/before/before_runbar-overlap_960x1080.png; scratchpad/verify/fix/item7/shots/before.json.
- **Done when**: No run bar control is covered at any width (the reason wraps in its own row, the skill's §2), with a browser test of the buttons' hit test at 960×1080, 1280×900, 390×844 and 1850×960.
- **Sources**: the T08 fix lane (scratchpad/t08fix/U1/pw/runbar.cjs); the verification brief, item 7
- **Closed at**: fd313af. Root: the reason's slot (`#shep-train-reason`) had `min-width: 0`, so it shrank below its words (its longest word and the GPU tag) and the line, which cannot be narrower than them, overflowed onto the buttons. Fix: the slot is never narrower than its words (`min-width: min-content`), so where they do not fit beside the controls the line takes a row of its own; a word longer than the row breaks. The Continue drawer's footer reason takes the same rule. Test: `test_training_page.py::test_no_run_bar_control_is_under_the_reason_at_any_window` (fails on fcc3dea at 960×1080); the shared UI walk passes. After the fix at 960×1080 the reason sits beside Start (x 358–712, Start x 726–803); at 390×844 it is a row of its own above the buttons. Screens: scratchpad/verify/fix/item7/shots/before_runbar_{960x1080,1280x900,390x844,1850x960}.png and after_runbar_*.png (whole windows: before_*.png, after_*.png).
- **Re-checked (verification, fcc3dea, part RB2)**: V-RB2-1 found the same reason over Start at 390×844 and its text outside its block at 1280×900, 1366×620 and 1536×760, touching the Run name box. Fixed by fd313af: measured on the served dashboard (operator, no workspaces folder) at 390×844, 1280×900, 1366×620, 1536×760, 960×1080 and 1850×960, the reason's text lies outside its block in 11–13 line boxes and over Start, Continue… or the Run name at fcc3dea, in none after, and each control's centre is its own (scratchpad/verify/fix/r2/runbar/{before,after}.json, shots/{before,after}_runbar_*.png).
- **Re-checked (review of 5033d29, 2026-11-17)**: "A word longer than the row breaks" above was not true at fd313af: `overflow-wrap: break-word` does not lower a word's min-content, and `min-width: min-content` sized the slot to its longest word. For an operator whose one workspace is named hospital_a_rare_disease_cohort_2026_11_16_reimport_v3 (not trainable), the reason spanned x 33–445 at 390×844 while the run bar's content ends at 357, every line cut at the panel's edge; an 80-character word in the GPU lock's reason gave x 33–745. Start's hit test held. Fixed at 9fa4fc2: one rule for both reason slots (the run bar's and the Continue drawer's footer's): as wide as their words (`flex-basis: min-content`), never under their floor beside the controls (min(120 px, 100%); the drawer's 240 px) and never wider than their row; the reason line is never wider than its slot, so such a word breaks inside it. Test: `test_training_page.py::test_no_run_bar_control_is_under_the_reason_at_any_window` now also runs the long name and checks every line of the reason inside the run bar and no sideways scroll at the four windows (fails at 5033d29 at 390×844; on fcc3dea both cases fail at 960×1080). Measured for both reasons at 960×1080, 1280×900, 390×844 and 1850×960: as at 5033d29 except at 390×844, where the reason now spans x 33–357 (scratchpad/verify/fix/vr/r1/before_*.json and after_*.json; shots/{before,after}_{gpu_lock,long_name}_{390x844,1850x960}.png). The evidence path in "At fcc3dea" was corrected (item7/shots/before.json).

#### V-17 · An input method's composition left open becomes the value Start sends

- **State**: awaiting owner acceptance · found by the review of 5033d29 · severity low · page: training
- **Problem**: A composition left uncommitted when Start is pressed is ended by the browser with what it holds (pinyin letters, say), and Start sends that as the field's value. A field with a format check refuses it (Neighbours per hop); a free-text field takes it, and the run starts with it.
- **At fcc3dea**: In Training's Run name, the composition "b", "ba", "bai" left open and Start pressed: the browser ended it as "bai", Start sent run_name "bai" and the job runner was asked to start a run named "bai" (the review's probe on 5033d29, whose touch script is fcc3dea's: scratchpad/verify/review/probe/test_review_ime_run_name.py, ime_run_name.log). fe6a8ce's test and message said "a half value never reaches a run", which holds only for a field whose own check refuses it; the test's words now say so.
- **Done when**: The owner or the integrator decides whether Start may send a composition its own press ended. If not, Start's held-click path (`TOUCH_HEAD`) treats an open composition as a pending edit (the press does not end it and nothing goes out until it is committed, or the refusal is said beside Start), with a browser test on Run name; if so, the decision is recorded here.
- **Sources**: the review of 5033d29 (R-3); the verification brief's IME item (A26)
- **Decided (integrator, 2026-11-17)**: Start may send a composition its own press ended. The press takes focus, the browser ends the composition with what it holds (Chromium, as observed), the field then shows that text, and Start sends what the field shows. This is the rule already decided for every pending edit: Start waits for it and carries it (OPS-R1, the r1b round). A26 asks that touched, null and the submitted value stay right, and they do: the value sent is the field's value, and a field's own check still refuses a value it does not take (Neighbours per hop). Holding Start while a composition is open would be a new behaviour with new words beside Start; the brief's "not submitted as a half value" read more into A26 than it says. The owner may still ask for the hold.
- **Closed at**: —
